# Stage 9 macros (group S9) for paper v6

Generated by `paper/make_figures.py` (run with `S9_MACRO_INDEX=docs/stage9/S9_MACROS.md`); do not edit by hand. 2511 macros, all new (no earlier macro changed). Source: `results/gpu_stage9` (the frozen scorer's `stage9_summary.json`, `fig_window.csv`, `fig_edits.csv` and `lb/<tag>.json`); every value is formatted as `STAGE9_SCORE.txt` prints it and asserted, with its printed context, on the score-file line given as its source.

## Conventions

- Names: `Nq` + statistic + format + block or onset word + model tag (`MistralSeven`, `LlamaEight`, `QwenSeven`). `...Lo` and `...Hi` are the 95 % two-stage cluster-bootstrap bounds. A name ending in `Min` or `Max` (or with `Min` / `Max` before a model tag) selects the smallest or largest of the listed registered strings; its meaning names which.
- Formats: stories `None` (no mention, `\fmtNone`), `Before` (`\fmtBefore`), `PostNull` (`\fmtNull`), `Post` (`\fmtPost`), `After` (the list after the story, `\fmtListA`), `Letter` (`\fmtLetter`); passages `Nom` (free form), `Optb` (options before), `Opta` (options after); edit rows `Nat` (the natural patch), `Eone` (E1, single site), `Efourb` (E4b, the trained DAS remap; not run at Llama), `Caa` (all-position steering); blocks `Three`, `Seven`, `Eleven`, `Fifteen`; passage onsets `Fifteen`, `Thirty`, `Fortyfive`, `Sixty` = round(x L) for x = 0.15, 0.3, 0.45, 0.6.
- Two precisions, as the scorer prints them: the verdict cells (PREDICTIONS, FALSIFIERS, DIRECTION B) have 3 decimals; the reported curves and edit tables have 2 (their l_c / L and silent-failure values have 3). Where one quantity is printed at both precisions it has two names, e.g. `\NqPthreeEarlyAfterMistralSeven` (P3s cell, 3 decimals) and `\NqEarlyAfterMistralSeven` (the curve in the figure, 2 decimals). Cite the 3-decimal name with a verdict and the 2-decimal name when describing the curves; do not mix the two for one number.
- Negative values (`\NqEoneDiff...`, `\NqEoneGap...`) carry a plain minus: use them in math mode. Values that round to zero are unsigned. Percentages: `\pct{\NqLcFracNoneMistralSeven}` (main.tex) prints 69 %.
- Verdict macros (`\NqVerdict...`, `\NqFalsifier...`) hold the scorer's words verbatim (e.g. `NOT SUPPORTED`, `SUPPORTED AT THE POINT`, `TRIGGERED`, `not triggered`); in running text write the verdict in words and use the macros only where a verbatim verdict is wanted.
- `[arithmetic on printed values]`: a count or ratio of printed values (l_b / L, cells within 0.1, totals); `[selects a printed string]`: a range or a decisive cell; every other macro is a printed string.
- Readings the outcome selects (docs/stage9/OUTCOME.md): passages are an evaluation warning only (F2 triggered at Qwen); the window claim is for single-site edits, patching and KV-cache edits (CAA transfers); the dose convexity has no threshold claim; the failed stage-8 channel-ratio law stays reported as failed.

Figure `paper/figures/fig_window.pdf` (draft caption): Whether a patch at the writing token still changes the answer depends on the depth and the format. (a-c) Share of generated answers changed to S by the late patch (S's key and value at the writing token in blocks l..L-1; solid) and by the early patch (blocks 0..l-1; dashed), against the onset l as a fraction of the depth L, for no mention, list before, sentence after, list after and letters after (N9, 150 stories; 95 % intervals as bands; dotted line: l_b). (d) Transfer to list after of edits written at blocks 3-15: the natural patch, single-site steering E1, the trained remap E4b (not run at Llama) and all-position steering (CAA); within a block, Mistral-7B, Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B from left to right (E9, 80 cores).

Table `paper/tables/tab_stage9.tex`: the verdict table (set it in a `table*`; caption and label in the section file).
